After all, the word Mestakung was first born in the world of physics, from the observation that in nature, something at a critical point will organize itself to find a new equilibrium; the term is self-organizing criticality. ### 🤖 ChatGPT:
Here is a polished, smoother, and more natural version while keeping your message intact:

---

When people hear the phrase *Mestakung for Business*, some may wonder: *What does “seMESTA menDUKUNG” (“the universe continuously supports”) have to do with the world of business?* Isn’t it a term from science, or perhaps something more philosophical?  
It’s a perfectly valid question.

The term *Mestakung* was indeed born in physics—from the observation that in nature, systems at a critical point will reorganize themselves to reach a new equilibrium, a phenomenon known as **self-organizing criticality**.  

But Mestakung is not confined to the realm of science. It is a mirror of life itself.

### 🤖 ChatGPT:
Istilah **“ngeri-ngeri sedap”** tidak punya padanan satu kata dalam bahasa Inggris, tetapi bisa diterjemahkan dengan beberapa pilihan sesuai konteks.

Berikut terjemahan yang paling mendekati:

### Pilihan Terjemahan
1. **“Thrilling yet terrifying moments”**  
   (momen yang mendebarkan tapi menakutkan)

2. **“Exciting but scary times”**

3. **“Moments that are both scary and delightful”**  
   — kalau ingin menangkap rasa “ngeri tapi malah bikin senang”.

4. **“Deliciously frightening moments”**  
   — ini lebih ekspresif, menangkap nuansa humor dan ketegangan dalam “ngeri-ngeri sedap”.

### Pilihan paling natural:
Jika konteksnya umum, yang paling alami adalah:

👉 **“Exciting but scary times”**

Jika konteksnya lebih dramatis atau emosional:

👉 **“Thrilling yet terrifying moments”**

Kalau mau rasa ekspresif khas Indonesia:

👉 **“Deliciously frightening”**

Boleh saya tahu konteks kalimatnya nanti supaya bisa dibuat paling pas?

### 👤 User:
apa yang kurang dari speech ini? MORAL RESPONSIBILITY TO BUILD AI THAT SERVES AND UPLIFTS HUMANITY
Opening Remarks by Luhut Binsar Pandjaitan
Chairman, National Economic Council of Indonesia
X International Scholas Chairs Congress – Vatican, 17 November 2025

1. INTRODUCTION
Your Holiness Pope Leo XIV,
Your Eminences,
Your Excellencies, Distinguished Scholars, and Members of Scholas Occurrentes,

It is a profound honor for me and for Indonesia to join this important gathering in the Vatican. Scholas Occurrentes has long united education, culture, and dialogue. Today, we meet at a moment of historic transformation.

2. THE PROMISE AND MORAL RESPONSIBILITY OF AI
Artificial Intelligence brings enormous promise.It can accelerate scientific discovery, expand access to education, transform healthcare, and elevate human creativity.
But with this promise comes a profound moral responsibility. AI can also distort truth, widen inequality, and reduce people to data if not guided by conscience and ethical purpose.
Over a century ago, Rerum Novarum addressed the moral challenges of the industrial revolution. Its warning is even more urgent now: technological progress must uphold human dignity and protect the vulnerable.
The autonomous systems of today require not only innovation, but moral clarity.
AI must always remain a tool for humanity, grounded in justice, compassion, and solidarity

3. BUILDING AI THAT SERVES AND UPLIFTS HUMANITY
To ensure AI uplifts society, we must build it with intention and meaning—not only efficiency.
AI should support learning, expand opportunity, and strengthen human relationships. As Pope Francis reminded us during his visit to Indonesia: technology must not dehumanize people.
AI must illuminate human potential—not overshadow it. And therefore, we must place ethical guardrails around its development and ensure that it nurtures education, dialogue, and inclusion.

4. INCLUSIVE AI AND TECHNOLOGICAL DEVELOPMENT
For AI to truly serve humanity, it must be inclusive. Brilliance can emerge from rural villages as easily as from global capitals.
Indonesia’s national motto Bhinneka Tunggal Ika—Unity in Diversity—reminds us that true unity is achieved when every community can contribute.
The “Brain Factory” at IT Del
At Institut Teknologi Del in rural North Sumatra, we built a model that opens the doors for learning technology to students from underserved backgrounds. Albeit still small, IT DEL now has one of the most advanced Research and Development in AI in Indonesia.
From this remote region, graduates have gone on to MIT, Harvard, Tsinghua, and other leading universities.
Their success shows that the future of AI should not be shaped only by those in privileged centers of power. It must also include the talent, resilience, and creativity of students from the rural and under-privileged areas.

5. EDUCATION AS A TOOL TO UPLIFT HUMANITY
The rise of AI makes education more important—not less. High-quality education gives young people the tools to shape technology with humanity.
The GASING Method: A Gift from Indonesia
Created by Professor Yohanes Surya from IT DEL, the GASING method of learning mathematics—joyful, intuitive, and simple; through games and singing —has transformed lives in some of Indonesia’s most remote provinces.


In Papua, children who once struggled with basic arithmetic mastered three-digit calculations in just two weeks through GASING.
GASING proves that human genius is universal, even if opportunity is not.
And it shows how education can uplift entire communities and prepare them for an AI-enabled world.

6. CLOSING
Your Holiness, Your Excellencies, Distinguished Guests,
This Congress is timely and necessary. AI is reshaping humanity faster than institutions can adapt. We share a moral responsibility to guide it with wisdom, compassion, and justice.
Indonesia stands ready to work with the Holy See, with Scholas Occurrentes, and with all nations to ensure that AI becomes a force that honors human dignity, promotes inclusion, strengthens education, and uplifts humanity.

Grazie. Thank you for your invitation to this very important congress.
May God bless us and guide our efforts for the good of humanity.

### 🤖 ChatGPT:
Berikut evaluasi **apa yang kurang** dari speech tersebut, plus **saran konkret** agar speech menjadi lebih kuat, lebih menyentuh, dan lebih efektif untuk audiens di Vatikan dan komunitas Scholas Occurrentes.

Saya membaginya menjadi 5 area:

---

# **1. Kurang “Personal Touch” dan Kisah Emosional**
Pidato ini sangat baik, rapi, dan terstruktur, tetapi *terasa agak formal dan teknokratis*. Untuk konteks **Vatikan**, **Paus**, dan **Scholas**, audiens akan lebih tersentuh jika ada:

### ✔️ Kisah pribadi  
Misalnya pengalaman Bapak Luhut sendiri melihat bagaimana teknologi membantu (atau merusak) nilai kemanusiaan.

### ✔️ Kisah kemanusiaan di Indonesia  
Speech sudah menyebut Papua dan GASING — bagus.  
Namun bisa diperkuat dengan satu ilustrasi manusiawi:

> “Saya pernah melihat seorang anak di Papua yang sebelumnya tidak percaya diri… lalu dalam dua minggu dia tersenyum karena mampu melakukan perhitungan tiga digit.”

Hal seperti ini membuat speech lebih “hidup”.

---

# **2. Kurang Jembatan Emosional dengan Paus Leo XIV / Paus Francis**
Karena konteksnya Vatikan dan Scholas Occurrentes, akan sangat kuat bila:

### ✔️ Mengutip atau menyinggung *Fratelli Tutti*, *Laudato Si’*, atau *Laudate Deum*  
Teks sudah menyebut Rerum Novarum — bagus.  
Tapi menambah satu referensi dokumen Paus zaman modern akan menunjukkan keharmonisan nilai.

Contoh:

> “As Pope Francis wrote in *Fratelli Tutti*, progress without compassion becomes a form of regression.”

---

# **3. Kurang Roadmap atau Komitmen Konkret**
Saat bicara AI dan moral responsibility, audiens biasanya ingin tahu **apa langkah praktis selanjutnya**.

Saran:

- komitmen Indonesia untuk standar etika AI  
- kerja sama pendidikan digital dengan Scholas  
- proyek inklusi digital rural  
- riset AI yang human-centered

Misalnya:

> “Indonesia is prepared to collaborate on a global ethical framework for AI that protects the young and empowers the poor.”

---

# **4. Kurang Kalimat Penutup yang Menggetarkan**
Penutup sudah baik, tetapi bisa dibuat **lebih memorable**, dengan satu kalimat kuat yang menjadi highlight.

Contoh:

> “Technology must reflect the best of our humanity—or it will amplify the worst.”

Atau:

> “Let us ensure that AI does not eclipse the human soul, but magnifies it.”

---

# **5. Alur Bisa Lebih Mulus dengan Satu Narasi Pengikat**
Pidato sudah sangat terstruktur, namun masing-masing bagian terasa seperti blok yang berdiri sendiri.

Untuk membuatnya lebih “flowing”, tambahkan *bridging sentences*.

Misalnya setelah bicara tentang IT DEL:

> “But education alone is not enough unless we anchor it in moral clarity.”

Atau sebelum bagian GASING:

> “Yet the heart of technology is not infrastructure—it is people.”

---

# **RINGKASAN: Apa yang Kurang**
✔ Kurang sentuhan personal dan kisah menyentuh  
✔ Kurang kutipan atau resonansi dengan ajaran Paus / dokumen Vatikan masa kini  
✔ Kurang komitmen konkret atau roadmap etika AI  
✔ Penutup bisa dibuat lebih powerful  
✔ Transisi antarbab bisa dibuat lebih halus

---

### 🔹 *Memberi dua versi*:  
- versi singkat (3 menit)  
- versi lengkap (6–8 menit)

# **MORAL RESPONSIBILITY TO BUILD AI THAT SERVES AND UPLIFTS HUMANITY**  
**Opening Remarks by Luhut Binsar Pandjaitan**  
Chairman, National Economic Council of Indonesia  
X International Scholas Chairs Congress – Vatican, 17 November 2025  

---

## **1. INTRODUCTION**

Your Holiness Pope Leo XIV,  
Your Eminences,  
Your Excellencies, Distinguished Scholars, and Members of Scholas Occurrentes,

It is a profound honor for me—and for Indonesia—to join this significant gathering here in the Vatican. Scholas Occurrentes has long united education, culture, and dialogue. Today, we meet at a moment of extraordinary transformation, when technology is reshaping the human journey at a pace never seen before.

This gathering is not only timely. It is necessary.

---

## **2. THE PROMISE AND MORAL RESPONSIBILITY OF AI**

Artificial Intelligence brings enormous promise. It can accelerate scientific discovery, expand access to education, improve healthcare, and elevate human creativity. It can give voices to the voiceless and open doors for communities long left behind.

But with this promise comes a profound moral responsibility.

If not guided by conscience, AI can distort truth, widen inequality, manipulate the vulnerable, and reduce human beings into mere data points. The human soul must never be an afterthought.

More than a century ago, *Rerum Novarum* warned that technological progress must always uphold human dignity and protect the vulnerable. The message is even more urgent today. As Pope Francis wrote in *Fratelli Tutti*, progress without compassion becomes a form of regression.

Today’s autonomous systems require not only innovation—but moral clarity.  
AI must remain a tool for humanity, grounded in justice, compassion, and solidarity.

---

## **3. BUILDING AI THAT SERVES AND UPLIFTS HUMANITY**

To ensure AI uplifts society, we must build it with intention—not merely with efficiency.

AI should support learning, expand opportunities, and strengthen human relationships. It must illuminate the human soul, not overshadow it.

During his visit to Indonesia, Pope Francis reminded us that technology must never dehumanize people. This is a message we hold close. Ethical guardrails are essential—not to limit progress, but to ensure progress remains humane.

The question before us is simple:  
**Will AI elevate human dignity, or erode it?**  
The answer depends on the choices we make now.

---

## **4. INCLUSIVE AI AND TECHNOLOGICAL DEVELOPMENT**

For AI to truly serve humanity, it must be inclusive. Brilliance can arise from rural villages just as much as global capitals. Talent is universal, opportunity is not.

Indonesia’s national motto *Bhinneka Tunggal Ika*—Unity in Diversity—reminds us that true unity is achieved when every community can contribute.

### **The “Brain Factory” at IT Del**

In rural North Sumatra, at Institut Teknologi Del, we built an educational model designed to open the doors of technology to students from underserved backgrounds. Although still small, IT Del now has one of the most advanced AI Research and Development centers in Indonesia.

From these remote village roads, young graduates have gone on to MIT, Harvard, Tsinghua, and other leading universities.

Their stories show that the future of AI must not be defined only by privileged centers of power, but also by the resilience and creativity of students from rural and underprivileged areas.

Inclusive AI is not charity—it is justice.

---

## **5. EDUCATION AS A TOOL TO UPLIFT HUMANITY**

As AI rises, education becomes even more essential. High-quality education gives young people not only skills, but values—the wisdom to shape technology with humanity.

### **The GASING Method: A Gift from Indonesia**

Created by Professor Yohanes Surya from IT Del, the GASING method—joyful, intuitive, simple, taught through games and songs—has transformed lives in some of Indonesia’s most remote provinces.

In Papua, I witnessed something unforgettable. Children who once struggled even with basic arithmetic became confident, smiling, and able to perform three-digit calculations in just two weeks. Their eyes shone with dignity.

GASING proves that human genius is universal—even when opportunity is not.  
It shows how education can uplift entire communities and prepare them to thrive in an AI-enabled world.

Education is the most powerful tool to ensure that technology elevates humanity rather than diminishes it.

---

## **6. A CALL TO GLOBAL COOPERATION**

Your Holiness, Your Excellencies, Distinguished Guests,

AI is advancing faster than institutions can adapt. This demands shared moral leadership.

Indonesia stands ready to work with the Holy See, with Scholas Occurrentes, and with nations across the world to ensure that:

- AI protects human dignity,  
- promotes inclusion,  
- strengthens education,  
- preserves cultural identity,  
- and uplifts humanity—especially the poor and the vulnerable.

Let us collaborate to build a global ethical framework for AI, one that reflects the richness of human conscience and the universality of compassion.

---

## **7. CLOSING**

Grazie. Thank you for your invitation and for this important congress.

May God bless our collective efforts so that humanity is not shaped by technology alone, but by wisdom, compassion, and the enduring belief that every person—every child—has infinite worth.

Let us ensure that AI does not eclipse the human soul,  
**but magnifies it.**

Thank you.  
May God bless us all.

---

Yohanes Surya’s involvement in the Physics Olympiad began when he was still a doctoral candidate in Physics at William & Mary. His interest was sparked when he saw an announcement that the 24th International Physics Olympiad (IPhO) would be held on the William & Mary campus. Together with his colleague, Agus Ananda, he contacted the University of Indonesia to help organize a selection for five Indonesian high school students.

At the end of 1994, he decided to return to Indonesia to train and lead the Indonesian Physics Olympiad Team (TOFI). In addition, he served as a lecturer and researcher in the postgraduate program at the University of Indonesia, specializing in nuclear physics (1995–1998).

Since 2000, Yohanes Surya has conducted numerous training programs for physics and mathematics teachers across Indonesia—from major cities to district capitals, small towns, villages, and even Islamic boarding schools. To facilitate these programs, he founded the Surya Institute. He also introduced the concepts of *Mestakung* (seMESTA menduKUNG) and *Gasing* (Easy, Fun, Enjoyable) to transform the learning of physics and mathematics.

### 👤 User:
apa beda critical state dan critical condition?

### 🤖 ChatGPT:
Perbedaan **critical state** dan **critical condition** cukup halus, tetapi penting—terutama jika ingin akurat secara ilmiah dan tetap enak dibaca dalam konteks tulisan seperti Mestakung.

Berikut penjelasan yang paling jelas dan sederhana:

---

# **1. Critical State**
**Critical state** biasanya menggambarkan **keadaan** atau **situasi menyeluruh** ketika suatu sistem berada di ambang perubahan besar.

🔹 Lebih ke *keadaan sistem secara keseluruhan*.  
🔹 Umumnya dipakai dalam fisika, kimia, dan teori sistem.  
🔹 Contoh: air di titik didih → “critical state of water molecules”.

**Maknanya:**  
Keadaan yang stabil-tapi-rapuh, siap berubah menjadi fase baru.

**Cocok untuk konsep Mestakung.**

---

# **2. Critical Condition**
**Critical condition** biasanya merujuk pada **syarat atau faktor penyebab** yang membuat sesuatu *menjadi kritis*.

🔹 Lebih ke *penyebab, batasan, atau kondisi pemicu*.  
🔹 Sering dipakai dalam konteks medis, teknik, atau syarat matematis.  
🔹 Contoh: “critical condition in the hospital”, atau “the critical condition for instability is…”

**Maknanya:**  
Kondisi spesifik yang memicu keadaan kritis.

---

# **Ringkasannya**
| Istilah | Fokus | Makna | Cocok untuk Mestakung? |
|--------|-------|--------|-------------------------|
| **Critical state** | Keadaan menyeluruh | Sistem berada di ambang perubahan | ✔ Sangat cocok |
| **Critical condition** | Syarat pemicu | Kondisi tertentu yang menyebabkan keadaan kritis | ◼ Bisa, tetapi kurang pas |

---

# **Untuk kalimat Anda, mana yang tepat?**

✔ **Critical state** lebih tepat, karena Anda bicara tentang *visi* yang membuat seseorang memasuki keadaan kritis yang mendorong perubahan besar.

---

- Jika dipadukan, AI dapat “berjiwa GASING.”

Nada ini sangat sinkron dengan isi pidato dan buku Anda.

### 🤖 ChatGPT:
Mengganti **“the intelligence of the heart”** dengan **“the human heart”** bisa dilakukan, tetapi **nuansanya berbeda**. Berikut penjelasannya agar Anda bisa memilih yang paling tepat untuk pesan Mestakung.

---

# 🔹 **1. “the intelligence of the heart”**  
**Kekuatan:**  
- Puitis  
- Mengandung makna bahwa hati juga “cerdas” (wisdom, compassion, clarity)  
- Selaras dengan konsep Mestakung: kesadaran, compassion, niat baik  
- Memberi kesan bahwa hati manusia adalah sebuah *agenti* atau *kapasitas* yang aktif membimbing AI.

**Nuansa:** spiritual, filosofis, mendalam.

**Contoh:**  
*“…and the intelligence of the heart helps AI become more human.”*

Ini memberi gambaran bahwa AI belajar dari kebijaksanaan hati.

---

# 🔹 **2. “the human heart”**  
**Kekuatan:**  
- Lebih sederhana  
- Lebih langsung  
- Lebih mudah dipahami oleh audiens global

**Namun:**  
- Tidak se-dalam “intelligence of the heart”  
- Kurang menampilkan aspek *kearifan, kejernihan, compassion*  
- Lebih terasa fisik atau emosional, bukan kesadaran

**Nuansa:** lebih literal, lebih dangkal maknanya.

**Contoh:**  
*“…and the human heart helps AI become more human.”*

Kalimat ini sedikit aneh, karena hati manusia “membuat AI menjadi manusia” terdengar terlalu literal.

---

# ⭐ **Rekomendasi Saya: Tetap gunakan “the intelligence of the heart.”**  
Karena:

- lebih sesuai dengan tema *consciousness*, *Mestakung*, *compassion*,  
- terdengar lebih indah dan kuat,  
- menghindari interpretasi literal yang membingungkan,  
- memberi kedalaman spiritual: hati memiliki kecerdasan yang memandu.

---

# 🔹 Jika ingin yang lebih sederhana, bisa pakai alternatif ini:
### **“the wisdom of the human heart”**  
Ini jembatan terbaik antara kedalaman dan kesederhanaan.

Contoh kalimat:

**“…AI helps us think, and the wisdom of the human heart helps AI become more human.”**

Ini sangat natural, kuat, dan tidak menimbulkan ambiguitas.

### 👤 User:
apa kata dalam bahasa inggri untuk menyatakan niat, keinginan yang menggebu-gebu

### 🤖 ChatGPT:
Untuk menyatakan **niat atau keinginan yang sangat menggebu-gebu** dalam bahasa Inggris, ada beberapa kata yang kuat dan tepat, tergantung nuansa yang diinginkan:

---

## **1. Determination**  
→ tekad kuat, niat yang tidak mudah goyah.  
**Nuansa:** fokus, tegas.  
**Contoh:** *strong determination*

## **2. Resolve**  
→ tekad yang mantap dan bulat.  
**Nuansa:** lebih dalam daripada *intention*.  
**Contoh:** *a burning resolve*

## **3. Aspiration**  
→ keinginan kuat yang bersifat ideal atau tinggi.  
**Nuansa:** penuh cita-cita.  
**Contoh:** *a powerful aspiration*

## **4. Passion**  
→ dorongan hati yang sangat kuat.  
**Nuansa:** emosional, menggebu-gebu.  
**Contoh:** *a deep passion to achieve*

## **5. Drive**  
→ dorongan internal yang kuat untuk bertindak.  
**Nuansa:** energik, penuh tenaga.  
**Contoh:** *a strong inner drive*

## **6. Burning desire**  
→ keinginan yang membara.  
**Nuansa:** paling tepat untuk “menggebu-gebu.”  
**Contoh:** *a burning desire to create change*

---

# 🔥 **Rekomendasi terbaik untuk “niat yang menggebu-gebu”:**
### **a burning desire**  
atau  
### **a burning resolve**

Keduanya sangat kuat, emotif, dan tepat.

### 🤖 ChatGPT:
Great question — **“shakes”** sebenarnya sudah sangat kuat dan natural dalam bahasa Inggris untuk menggambarkan sesuatu yang **mengguncang**, **mengoyahkan**, **mengganggu kestabilan batin**.

Namun jika Anda ingin lebih dekat ke “mengganggu” atau “menggoyahkan” secara halus, berikut pilihan katanya:

---

# 🔹 **1. “unsettles”** (paling halus dan paling tepat untuk menggoyahkan hati)
Makna: membuat tidak tenang, menggoyahkan dari dalam.  
Nuansa: emosional, spiritual, sangat cocok untuk tulisan Anda.

**Contoh:**  
*It arrives suddenly, unsettles us, and forces us to choose: stop or rise.*

Ini sangat pas dengan makna “menggoyahkan batin.”

---

# 🔹 **2. “disturbs”**
Makna: mengganggu.  
Nuansa: lebih kasar dan lebih negatif dibanding “unsettles.”  
Kurang tepat untuk gaya puitis buku Anda.

**Contoh:**  
*It arrives suddenly, disturbs us…*  
→ terdengar seperti “mengganggu secara kasar,” kurang elegan.

---

# 🔹 **3. “shakes us”**
Makna: mengguncang.  
Nuansa: kuat, dramatis, mudah dipahami.  
Sangat natural dalam bahasa Inggris.

Jika Anda ingin tetap kuat dan simbolik, gunakan ini.

---

# ⭐ **Rekomendasi terbaik untuk gaya Mestakung:**  
### **“unsettles us”**  
Karena lembut, batiniah, dan sesuai dengan tujuan Anda.

---

# Pilihan kalimat paling elegan:

**Failure tests our hearts the moment we take a step.  
It arrives suddenly, unsettles us, and forces us to choose: stop or rise.**

Dan pada bagian selanjutnya:

**Failure unsettles the steps we are taking.  
Technology unsettles the direction we choose.**

Ini sangat harmonis dan puitis.

### 🤖 ChatGPT:
Untuk **“AI Berjiwa GASING”**, tidak ada terjemahan literal dalam bahasa Inggris yang langsung tepat, karena ini adalah konsep khas Indonesia yang menggabungkan *Gampang, Asyik, Menyenangkan*. Terjemahannya harus menangkap **roh** dan **spirit** GASING, bukan hanya kata-katanya.

Berikut beberapa pilihan terbaik, tergantung nuansa yang ingin Anda tonjolkan:

---

# ⭐ **1. “GASING-spirited AI”** *(paling kuat & paling identitas)*  
- Menjaga istilah GASING sebagai brand.  
- “spirited” memberi makna: punya jiwa, energi, kehangatan.  
Cocok untuk buku, pidato, poster, atau branding.

---

# ⭐ **2. “AI with the spirit of GASING”** *(paling jelas & mudah dipahami)*  
- Menyebutkan GASING secara langsung.  
- Tidak membingungkan pembaca internasional.  
- Jelas menegaskan bahwa GASING adalah konsep tersendiri.

---

# ⭐ **3. “GASING-minded AI”**  
- Memberi kesan AI yang mengikuti pola pikir GASING: mudah, fun, jelas.  
- Lebih teknis, cocok untuk konteks edukasi/AI learning.

---

# ⭐ **4. “Compassionate and joyful AI”** *(versi makna, bukan literal)*  
- Bila ingin menekankan *asyik, menyenangkan, penuh kasih*, tanpa harus menyebut GASING.  
- Tetapi kehilangan identitas GASING sebagai brand Indonesia.

---

# 🎯 **Rekomendasi utama untuk konteks internasional (misalnya Vatican, buku global):**

### **“AI with the spirit of GASING.”**

Alasannya:  
- jelas,  
- elegan,  
- menjaga merek GASING,  
- mudah dipahami oleh pembaca luar negeri,  
- terasa filosofis dan hangat.

terjemahkan: Peluang Besar Indonesia di Era Revolusi 5.0 

Ketika mesin belajar, ia memang akan menjadi semakin cerdas. Tetapi pertanyaan kuncinya: 

“Dilatih dengan jiwa seperti apa?” 

Di sinilah Indonesia punya peluang emas. Kita memiliki dua hal yang sangat istimewa: 

GASING 
Metode pembelajaran yang gampang, asyik, menyenangkan; penuh compassion, dengan langkah-langkah jelas yang membuat anak merasa mampu dan bahagia. 

INAAI 
Language model bahasa Indonesia yang bisa dilatih dengan karakter, budaya, dan nilai kedaulatan bangsa, dibentuk dengan prinsip-prinsip GASING. 

 

Jika dunia bergerak menuju Revolusi 5.0—era ketika manusia dan AI hidup berdampingan sebagai mitra—Indonesia bisa menyumbangkan sesuatu yang unik yaitu AI yang dibesarkan seperti anak-anak Indonesia dididik oleh guru-guru GASING. 

 

Bagaimana GASING Melatih Guru, dan Bagaimana AI Bisa Belajar Hal yang Sama 

Metode GASING telah melahirkan transformasi dahsyat yang tersebar dari Aceh hingga Papua. 

Guru Batak yang dulu keras kini mengajar dengan kelembutan dan senyum. 

Guru Manado yang dulu dijuluki “Ibu Monster” kini menjadi guru yang dirindukan murid-muridnya. 

Guru Morotai yang dulu ditakuti kini menjadi tempat murid menemukan ketenangan. 

Guru Jawa yang semula terlalu lembut kini menjadi lebih kreatif dan lincah. 

Guru Papua yang dulu masuk kelas membawa rotan kini masuk membawa hati yang penuh kasih. 

Apa yang mereka alami? 

Mereka berubah karena GASING bukan sekadar metode mengajar. GASING adalah getaran-getaran kasih, tawa, kepercayaan, kesabaran, dan harapan. 

GASING melatih 8C: 

Compassion (peduli dari hati) 

Character (keteguhan nia) 

Creativity (cara pandang baru) 

Critical Thinking (logika yang jernih) 

Collaboration (belajar bersama) 

Communication (bahasa yang menguatkan) 

Computational Logic (pola langkah jelas) 

Culture (kearifan lokal Indonesia) 

Dan yang paling penting: 

GASING mengubah “aku tidak bisa” menjadi “aku MAMPU” melalui perjalanan kecil bertahap. Zero to hero. 

Jika prinsip ini ditanamkan ke dalam AI, maka lahirlah generasi baru kecerdasan buatan: AI berjiwa GASING. 

 

AI Berjiwa GASING: Mesin yang Belajar dari Cinta, Bukan dari Ketakutan 

AI seperti ini bukan sekadar cerdas. Ia berjiwa. 

Ia: 

belajar dari kasih sayang, bukan ancaman, 

melihat kesalahan bukan sebagai hukuman, tetapi sinyal untuk membimbing, 

meniru guru GASING yang tersenyum dan berkata: “Coba lagi… kamu pasti bisa.” 

 

Ciri-cirinya: 

Mengasihi, bukan menghakimi 

Memampukan tanpa paksaan 

Setiap anak punya ritmenya sendiri. 

Ada yang cepat, ada yang perlu waktu. 

AI tidak memaksa. Ia menemani, mengikuti alur alami pertumbuhan seorang anak. 

Sabar dan setia 

AI memberi umpan balik lembut, bukan tekanan.  

Setiap rekomendasi adalah dorongan halus: “Ayo maju satu langkah lagi.” 

Adil bagi setiap pribadi 

Tidak semua anak sama. 

AI berjiwa GASING menyesuaikan pendekatannya untuk tiap anak, bukan memaksa mereka patuh pada angka dan standar yang kaku. 

Menyimpan jejak tawa, bukan sekadar data 

Data bukan hanya angka nilai dan statistik, tetapi juga pola kebahagiaan: kapan anak tersenyum, kapan ia menemukan minat baru, kapan kepercayaan dirinya tumbuh. 

AI seperti ini memiliki jiwa bukan karena diberi kode moral, tetapi karena dilatih dalam lingkungan kasih sayang, lingkungan yang telah menjadi DNA dari metode GASING.

## **Indonesia’s Great Opportunity in the Era of Revolution 5.0**

When machines learn, they will indeed become increasingly intelligent.  
But the key question is:

**“With what kind of soul are they trained?”**

This is where Indonesia holds a golden opportunity.  
We have two extraordinary gifts:

---

### **GASING**  
A learning method that is easy, fun, and joyful—full of compassion, with clear steps that make children feel capable and happy.

### **INAAI**  
An Indonesian language model that can be trained with the character, culture, and values of our nation, shaped with the principles of GASING.

---

If the world is moving toward Revolution 5.0—an era in which humans and AI live side by side as partners—Indonesia can contribute something truly unique:  
**AI raised the same way Indonesian children are taught by GASING teachers.**

---

## **How GASING Trains Teachers, and How AI Can Learn the Same Things**

The GASING method has created remarkable transformations across Indonesia, from Aceh to Papua.

A Batak teacher who was once strict now teaches with gentleness and a smile.  
A Manado teacher once called “Madam Monster” is now the teacher her students long for.  
A Morotai teacher who used to be feared has become a place where students find peace.  
A Javanese teacher who was once too gentle has become more creative and dynamic.  
A Papuan teacher who used to enter class with a stick now walks in with a heart full of love.

What happened to them?

They changed because GASING is not just a teaching method.  
**GASING is a vibration of love, laughter, trust, patience, and hope.**

GASING trains the **8Cs**:

- Compassion (caring from the heart)  
- Character (strength of intention)  
- Creativity (new ways of seeing)  
- Critical Thinking (clear logic)  
- Collaboration (learning together)  
- Communication (uplifting language)  
- Computational Logic (clear step-by-step patterns)  
- Culture (the wisdom of Indonesian local traditions)

And most importantly:

**GASING turns “I can’t” into “I CAN” through small, gradual steps. Zero to hero.**

If these principles are embedded in AI, a new generation of artificial intelligence will emerge:

## **GASING-spirited AI.**

---

## **GASING-Spirited AI: Machines That Learn From Love, Not From Fear**

AI like this is not merely intelligent.  
**It has a soulfulness.**

It:

- learns from compassion, not threat,  
- sees mistakes not as punishment, but as signals for guidance,  
- imitates a GASING teacher who smiles and says: *“Try again… you can do it.”*

---

### **Its characteristics:**

**Loving, not judging**  
It empowers without force.

**Honors each child’s rhythm**  
Some learn fast, others need time.  
AI does not push—it accompanies, following the natural flow of a child’s growth.

**Patient and faithful**  
AI gives gentle feedback, not pressure.  
Every recommendation is a soft nudge: *“Let’s take one more step forward.”*

**Fair to every individual**  
Not all children are the same.  
GASING-spirited AI adapts to each child, instead of forcing them to fit rigid numbers and standards.

**Storing moments of joy, not just data**  
Data is not only test scores and statistics—it also includes patterns of happiness:  
when a child smiles, when they discover a new interest, when their confidence grows.

AI like this has a “soul” not because it is given moral codes,  
but because it is trained in an environment of love—  
the same environment that has become the DNA of the GASING method.

Kasih adalah energi yang menyebar.

## **Quantum Compassion: Love That Spreads Like Light**

GASING-spirited AI brings a new kind of power: **Quantum Compassion.**

The term sounds scientific, but the meaning is simple:

**A small act of genuine kindness can trigger a wave of goodness far larger than itself.**

In physics, a quantum is a tiny packet of energy carried by a particle of light (a photon).  
One photon seems insignificant.  
But when trillions of photons move together, the sunlight that warms the Earth is born.

Kindness works in exactly the same way.

A single smile, a single encouragement, a simple “you can do it” may seem small.  
But when millions of these small acts spread from one person to another, they create a social wave that transforms lives.

This is the essence of **Quantum Compassion**:

**Love is energy that spreads.**  
**Love connects hearts through an invisible network.**  
**Love creates new order in the midst of chaos.**  
**Small acts of love give birth to great change.**

Every sincere action—helping, uplifting, teaching patiently—is a “quantum” that nudges the world toward a new balance.  
And when AI learns to reflect this resonance of compassion, it becomes an amplifier of goodness.

Big countries may have the most advanced technology,

## **The Era of Digital Mestakung: When Technology Begins to Feel**

“Machines will never be wiser than their creators.”

Imagine a morning in the future.  
A Papuan child sits in front of a small tablet, practicing arithmetic.  
There is no angry teacher beside him, no harsh voice, no pressure.

Only a gentle voice is heard:

**“Try again slowly… You can do it.”**

The voice does not come from a human.  
It comes from **GASING-spirited AI**, an artificial intelligence trained not only with data, but with compassion and patience.

This AI does not scold when the child makes a mistake.  
It encourages him when he starts to feel tired.  
It knows when to offer a challenge, and when to give a digital “hug” through words like:

**“Excellent! Keep going.”**

A small smile appears on the child's face—  
a smile that used to be rare whenever math class began.

At that moment, something beautiful is happening:  
**Technology and humanity are resonating together.**

This is what we call **Digital Mestakung**—  
when the universe works through both machines and humans to bring greater goodness into the world.

Throughout civilization, every leap in technology was born not because humans were strong, but because human **awareness** evolved.

The compass was born from the need to find direction.  
The telescope was born from the longing to see farther.  
The computer was born from the dream of understanding how the mind works.

So the question is no longer:

**“How smart are the machines we build?”**

The more important question is:

**“How clear is our consciousness when we build them?”**

Because technology is never neutral.  
It always inherits the soul of its creator.

Social media was built with the intention of keeping people on the platform.  
When that intention was not accompanied by wisdom, the result appeared in the form of addiction, division, and emotional distress.

The technology itself was not evil.  
It was the **container of consciousness** that needed cleansing.

In the GASING tradition, there are three core principles:

**Critical** — the courage to ask *why*, not just *what.*  
**Step** — the courage to act with good intention.  
**Persevere** — the ability to guard the heart in a world ruled by automation.

When these three values are practiced, every school, office, and laboratory can become a place where technology is created to humanize humanity.

Ia memberi semangat ketika anak mulai lesu.  

Ia tahu kapan harus memberi tantangan, dan kapan harus memberi pelukan digital berupa kata,  

“Bagus sekali! Lanjut, ya.” 

 

Di wajah anak itu muncul senyum kecil, senyum yang dulu jarang terlihat setiap kali pelajaran matematika dimulai.  

 

Pada saat itu, sesuatu yang indah sedang terjadi: Teknologi dan manusia sedang beresonansi.  

Inilah Digital Mestakung, ketika semesta bekerja melalui mesin dan manusia untuk membawa kebaikan yang lebih besar. 

Inilah peradaban tertinggi yang dirindukan orang.  

 

Sejak awal peradaban, setiap lompatan teknologi lahir bukan karena manusia kuat, tetapi karena kesadaran meningkat.  

 

Kompas muncul dari kebutuhan untuk mencari arah. 

Teleskop muncul dari kerinduan melihat lebih jauh. 

Komputer muncul dari mimpi memahami cara pikiran bekerja.  

 

Maka pertanyaannya bukan lagi,  

 

“Seberapa pintar mesin yang kita buat?”  

 

Pertanyaan yang lebih penting adalah,  

 

“Seberapa jernih kesadaran kita ketika membuatnya?”  

 

Sebab teknologi tidak pernah netral.  

Ia selalu mewarisi jiwa penciptanya.  

 

Media sosial dibuat dengan niat membuat orang tetap berada di dalam platform. Ketika niat itu tidak ditemani kebijaksanaan, hasilnya muncul dalam bentuk kecanduan, perpecahan, dan tekanan batin. 

Teknologinya bukan jahat. Wadah kesadaran manusianyalah yang perlu dibersihkan. 

 

Dalam tradisi GASING ada tiga prinsip  

Kritis - berani bertanya mengapa, bukan hanya apa.  

Langkah - berani bertindak dengan niat baik. 

Tekun setia menjaga hati di tengah dunia yang serba otomatis. 

 

Jika tiga nilai itu dijalankan, setiap sekolah, kantor, dan laboratorium dapat menjadi tempat lahirnya teknologi yang memanusiakan manusia.

**“A machine will never be wiser than its creator.”**

Imagine a morning in the future.  
A Papuan child sits in front of a small tablet, practicing arithmetic.  
There is no angry teacher beside him, no harsh voice, no pressure.

Only a gentle voice is heard:

**“Try again slowly… you can do it.”**

The voice does not come from a human.  
It comes from a **GASING-spirited AI**, an artificial intelligence trained not only with data, but with compassion and patience.

This AI does not scold when the child makes a mistake.  
It encourages him when he begins to feel tired.  
It knows when to offer a challenge, and when to give a “digital hug” through words like:

**“Excellent! Keep going.”**

A small smile appears on the child’s face—  
a smile that used to be rare whenever math class began.

In that moment, something beautiful is happening:  
**technology and humanity are resonating.**

This is **Digital Mestakung**—  
when the universe works through both humans and machines to bring forth greater goodness.  
This is the higher civilization we have long dreamed of.

Throughout history, every major leap in technology did not arise because humans grew stronger,  
but because **human awareness expanded.**

The compass was born from the need to find direction.  
The telescope from the longing to see farther.  
The computer from the dream of understanding how the mind works.

Therefore, the question is no longer:

**“How smart are the machines we build?”**

The more important question is:

**“How clear is our consciousness when we build them?”**

Technology is never neutral.  
It always inherits the soul of its creator.

Social media was built with the intention of keeping people on the platform.  
When that intention was not guided by wisdom, it led to addiction, division, and emotional exhaustion.

The technology itself is not evil.  
It is the **container of human consciousness** that must be purified.

In the GASING tradition, there are three principles:

**Critical** — the courage to ask *why*, not just *what.*  
**Step** — the courage to act with good intention.  
**Persevere** — staying true to the heart in a world ruled by automation.

When these three values are practiced, every school, office, and laboratory can become a birthplace of technologies that uplift humanity.

### 👤 User:
terjemahkan: Sains dan Teknologi di Era Digital Mestakung 

 

Digital Mestakung membuka babak baru dalam ilmu pengetahuan: manusia, AI, dan semesta bekerja dalam satu irama. 

Dulu, ilmuwan butuh bertahun-tahun menguji satu hipotesis. Kini, dengan bantuan AI: 

ribuan ide bisa diuji sekaligus, 

triliunan kombinasi data bisa disaring, 

pola yang tak terlihat mata manusia bisa ditemukan. 

 

Contoh nyata: 

AlphaFold (DeepMind) memecahkan struktur protein yang puluhan tahun membingungkan ilmuwan, AI melakukannya dalam waktu dua tahun. 

NASA memakai AI kuantum untuk membaca cuaca antariksa. 

CERN menggunakan AI untuk mengurai triliunan benturan partikel demi mencari jejak dark-matter. 

 

Dunia sains kini seperti memiliki “guru privat” yang tak pernah lelah, sabar, dan selalu ingin membantu menemukan kebenaran. 

 

PKC: Otak Digital Pribadi 

Sistem PKC (Personal Knowledge Container) yang dikembangkan Ben Koo akan menghubungkan jutaan orang dalam jejaring pengetahuan global. 

Dulu, pengetahuan hidup dalam file: terpisah, kaku, sulit terhubung. 
Dalam PKC, pengetahuan hidup seperti otak manusia: ide-ide saling terhubung, tumbuh, dan belajar. 

PKC memberi setiap orang semacam “otak digital pribadi” yang: 

menyimpan, 

menghubungkan, 

membantu memahami, 

dan memperkaya pemikiran. 

Bayangkan jutaan “otak digital pribadi” ini terhubung, ditambah AI berperasaan yang membantu mempercepat pemahaman. 
 

Hasilnya: jaringan pengetahuan raksasa yang mampu menemukan dan mencipta dengan kecepatan yang belum pernah terjadi dalam sejarah. 

Ketika ilmu, AI, dan kesadaran manusia bergerak bersama, Digital Mestakung bekerja pada tingkat tertinggi. 

### 🤖 ChatGPT:
Berikut terjemahan dalam bahasa Inggris yang **jelas, mengalir, kuat secara makna**, dan tetap mempertahankan keindahan konsep Digital Mestakung:

---

## **Science and Technology in the Era of Digital Mestakung**

Digital Mestakung opens a new chapter in knowledge:  
humans, AI, and the universe working in a single rhythm.

In the past, scientists needed years to test a single hypothesis.  
Now, with the help of AI:

• thousands of ideas can be tested at once,  
• trillions of data combinations can be filtered,  
• patterns invisible to the human eye can be discovered.

**Real examples:**

• AlphaFold (DeepMind) solved protein structures that puzzled scientists for decades—AI cracked them in just two years.  
• NASA uses quantum AI to read space weather.  
• CERN uses AI to analyze trillions of particle collisions in the search for dark matter.

Today, science feels as if it has a “personal tutor” that never tires—patient, precise, and always ready to help uncover truth.

---

## **PKC: The Personal Digital Brain**

The PKC system (Personal Knowledge Container), developed by Ben Koo, will connect millions of people in a global knowledge network.

In the past, knowledge lived inside files—separated, rigid, difficult to link.  
In PKC, knowledge lives like the human brain: ideas are connected, growing, and learning.

PKC gives each person their own “personal digital brain” that:

• stores,  
• connects,  
• helps interpret,  
• and enriches their thinking.

Imagine millions of these “digital brains” connected—  
supported by compassionate AI that accelerates understanding.

The result:  
a vast knowledge network capable of discovering and creating at speeds never seen before in human history.

When science, AI, and human consciousness move together,  
**Digital Mestakung works at its highest level.**

---

### 👤 User:
terjemahkan: Pemerintah dan Politik di Era Digital Mestakung 

 

Era Politik Baru: Ketika Pemerintahan Belajar Berperasaan 

Bayangkan dunia di mana keputusan politik terasa seperti keputusan seorang kakak atau guru yang benar-benar peduli. 

AI berjiwa GASING membantu pemerintahan: 

membaca sinyal emosional warga, 

mendeteksi stres sosial lebih awal, 

merekomendasikan kebijakan yang menyehatkan jiwa masyarakat. 

Batas negara tidak lagi hanya soal garis di peta, tetapi juga soal nilai. 

Pemerintahan Tanpa Batas Wilayah 

Di masa depan, batas negara tidak lagi ditentukan oleh garis di peta, tetapi oleh nilai yang dipegang manusia. 

Seorang guru Dayak di Kalimantan bisa “tinggal” dalam wilayah digital yang sama dengan peneliti Jepang yang melatih  AI belajar matematika dengan senyum. 

Mereka tidak satu negara, tetapi satu frekuensi hati. Bayangkan munculnya tiga “wilayah” baru: 

Wilayah Kasih 

Relawan, guru, perawat, dan siapa pun yang hidup untuk menolong orang lain berkumpul di sini. 

Wilayah Inovasi 

Ilmuwan, peneliti, dan para pencipta teknologi bersih bekerja sama memperbaiki bumi. 

Wilayah Kebijaksanaan 

Para pemikir, seniman, pendidik, dan tokoh spiritual memandu agar teknologi tetap selaras dengan hati manusia. 

Dalam dunia seperti ini, manusia tidak lagi “terpecah” karena perbedaan kebangsaan, tetapi “terhubung” karena nilai yang diyakini. 

Di sinilah konsep Bhinneka Tunggal Ika berevolusi menjadi Bhinneka Tunggal Ika Digital, suatu kesadaran baru bahwa perbedaan budaya dan asal justru memperkaya kolaborasi global. Sebuah dunia di mana kita terhubung bukan karena paspor, tetapi karena frekuensi niat dan kemanusiaan yang sama. 

 

Pemerintahan yang Hidup dan Belajar 

Pemerintahan masa depan akan seperti tubuh manusia: kalau ada bagian yang sakit, seluruh sistem langsung tahu dan bergerak. 

AI berperasaan akan membaca “sinyal emosional warga”. Contohnya: 

Jika banyak warga merasa stres, AI akan merekomendasikan jam kerja lebih pendek. 

Jika banyak anak tampak gelisah, sistem pendidikan akan menambah sesi kreativitas dan bermain. 

Jika sebuah komunitas mulai kehilangan harapan, AI akan mendorong kegiatan seni, budaya, dan penyembuhan sosial. 

Pemerintahan tidak kaku, tetapi hidup, peka, dan belajar seperti seorang ibu yang memahami anak-anaknya. 

 

Pemimpin yang Dipilih Karena Niat Baik 

Tidak ada kampanye, baliho, atau janji palsu. AI berjiwa GASING dapat membaca niat dan rekam jejak moral seseorang, seperti guru GASING memandang murid dengan mata kasih. 

Bayangkan seorang perempuan sederhana di Flores yang 20 tahun menjaga mata air desanya. AI melihat ketulusannya, komitmennya, dan dampaknya bagi masyarakat. Sistem pun “mengusulkan”: “Inilah pemimpin terbaik untuk menjaga air dunia.” 

Dunia masa depan tidak memilih pemimpin yang paling keras suaranya, tetapi yang paling lembut hatinya. 

 

Pada akhirnya, ukuran kemajuan negara pun bergeser. 
Bukan sekadar GDP, tetapi ROC – Return on Compassion: 

apakah anak belajar dengan bahagia, 

apakah lansia merasa dihargai, 

apakah alam terjaga, 

apakah masyarakat punya harapan. 

Negara dengan ROC tinggi bukan sekadar superpower, tetapi super-compassion power. 

### 🤖 ChatGPT:
Berikut terjemahan bahasa Inggris yang **kuat, jelas, puitis, dan tetap mudah dipahami**, sambil menjaga kedalaman makna konsep Digital Mestakung:

---

## **Government and Politics in the Era of Digital Mestakung**

### **A New Political Era: When Governance Learns to Feel**

Imagine a world where political decisions feel like the decisions of an older sibling or a teacher who truly cares.

GASING-spirited AI helps governments:

• read the emotional signals of citizens,  
• detect social stress early,  
• recommend policies that strengthen the emotional well-being of society.

Borders are no longer just lines on a map—but values held by people.

---

## **Borderless Governance**

In the future, national borders will no longer be defined by maps, but by the values people live by.

A Dayak teacher in Kalimantan might “live” in the same digital region as a Japanese researcher teaching AI to learn mathematics with a smile.

They may not share citizenship, but they share the same frequency of heart.

Imagine three new “territories” emerging:

### **The Territory of Compassion**  
Where volunteers, teachers, nurses, and anyone who lives to help others gather.

### **The Territory of Innovation**  
Where scientists, researchers, and creators of clean technology work together to heal the Earth.

### **The Territory of Wisdom**  
Where thinkers, artists, educators, and spiritual leaders ensure that technology stays aligned with the human heart.

In such a world, humanity is no longer “divided” by nationality, but **connected** by shared values.

This is where the concept of *Bhinneka Tunggal Ika* evolves into **Digital Bhinneka Tunggal Ika**—  
a new consciousness in which cultural and national differences enrich global collaboration.  
A world where we are connected not by passports, but by the same frequency of intention and humanity.

---

## **A Government That Lives and Learns**

The government of the future will work like the human body:  
when one part hurts, the whole system senses it and responds.

Compassionate AI will read the “emotional signals” of the people. For example:

• If many citizens feel stressed, AI recommends shorter working hours.  
• If children appear anxious, the education system increases creativity and playtime.  
• If a community begins to lose hope, AI encourages art, culture, and social healing activities.

Government becomes not rigid, but **alive, sensitive, and learning**—like a mother who understands her children.

---

## **Leaders Chosen for Their Good Intentions**

No campaigns, no billboards, no false promises.  
GASING-spirited AI can read a person’s intention and moral record,  
just as a GASING teacher sees students through eyes of compassion.

Imagine a humble woman in Flores who has protected her village’s spring water for 20 years.  
AI sees her sincerity, her commitment, and her impact.  
The system “suggests”:  
**“This is the best leader to protect the world’s water.”**

The future world does not choose leaders with the loudest voices—  
but those with the gentlest hearts.

---

## **A New Measure of National Progress**

In the end, the measure of a nation’s progress changes.  
Not merely GDP, but **ROC — Return on Compassion**:

• Are children learning with joy?  
• Do the elderly feel valued?  
• Is nature being protected?  
• Do people still have hope?

A nation with high ROC is not just a superpower—  
it becomes a **super-compassion power.**

---

### 👤 User:
terjemahkan: Bisnis di Era Digital Mestakung 

 

Bayangkan sebuah perusahaan di masa depan seperti sebuah kampung di Kalimantan. Di kampung itu, semua orang saling kenal, saling menyokong, dan setiap tindakan satu orang memengaruhi seluruh kampung. Begitulah bisnis di era Digital Mestakung. 

 

Bisnis bukan lagi mesin besar yang hanya menghitung untung-rugi. Ia berubah menjadi organisme hidup seperti tubuh manusia atau hutan yang bernapas bersama. Ia punya dua jantung: 

satu berdetak dengan data, 

satu lagi berdetak dengan kesadaran dan niat baik. 

Ketika dua jantung ini berdetak seirama, perusahaan tidak hanya berjalan cepat, tetapi juga berjalan benar. 

 

Perusahaan seperti ini tidak sekadar mengejar ROI (Return on Investment), berapa besar uang yang kembali setelah kita berinvestasi. Tapi sekarang, ada ukuran baru yang pelan-pelan mulai menggantikan pusat perhatian itu: ROC, Return on Compassion yang mengukur 

seberapa besar kebaikan, empati, dan kesejahteraan yang dihasilkan, 

berapa banyak senyum, rasa aman, dan harapan yang tumbuh. 

Contohnya  

Salesforce menyisihkan 1% saham, waktu, dan produk mereka untuk kegiatan sosial. 

Hasilnya? Mereka menjadi magnet bagi talenta terbaik di dunia karena orang ingin bekerja untuk perusahaan yang berhati. 

Patagonia memutuskan seluruh keuntungannya digunakan untuk menjaga bumi. 

Keputusan yang terdengar “gila” bagi bisnis biasa, tetapi justru membuat pelanggan makin setia. 

INAAI, startup asli Indonesia, mengembalikan sampai 20% profitnya untuk pendidikan, riset, dan pengembangan. 

Karena kami percaya: satu rupiah yang kembali ke ilmu pengetahuan akan menjadi cahaya yang menerangi masa depan banyak orang. 

Saat niat jernih, teknologi dan manusia mulai bergerak di frekuensi yang sama. Inilah Mestakung dalam bisnis: niat baik menjadi magnet yang menggerakkan semesta digital.

Pendidikan: Belajar Jadi Ruang Bahagia 

Bayangkan ruang kelas masa depan. Ada guru, ada murid, dan ada satu teman digital yang selalu tersenyum: AI berjiwa GASING. 

AI seperti ini tidak menggantikan guru, tetapi menjadi sahabat belajar yang membantu dengan penuh kasih. 

Ia tahu kapan seorang anak mulai bingung. 
Ia bisa “merasakan” ketika seorang murid kehilangan semangat. 
Ia menyisipkan dorongan kecil, “Ayo, kamu pasti bisa,” dengan nada lembut.

Sekolah pun berubah menjadi tempat di mana hati bertumbuh lebih dulu, dan otak mengikuti kemudian.

AI menjelaskan rumus, guru memberi makna dan kehangatan. 
Di era Digital Mestakung, pendidikan menjadi salah satu sektor tercantik, karena teknologi dan kasih sayang berjalan beriringan di dalam kelas. 

 

 

Kesehatan: Rumah Sakit yang Menyembuhkan Hati

nada suara pasien, 

ekspresi lelah, 

bahkan ritme napas yang berubah saat seseorang cemas. 

Dokter dan perawat tetap jadi pusat kemanusiaan, tapi AI membantu mereka melihat hal yang mata kadang tak menangkap. 

Rumah sakit tidak hanya mengobati penyakit, tetapi juga mengembalikan ketenangan. 

Mungkin suatu hari orang berkata: 

“Ane suka di rumah sakit, di sana hati terasa lebih ringan.” 

Itulah kesehatan di era Digital Mestakung, ketika data dan perasaan bekerja bersama. 

 

Energi & Lingkungan: Dari Menggali Tanah ke Menggali Kesadaran 

Selama ratusan tahun, manusia menggali bumi untuk energi. Tapi di era Digital Mestakung, arah ini bisa berubah. 

AI berjiwa GASING akan membantu manusia mendengar “bahasa alam”,  angin yang pelan berkata, matahari yang menyala hangat, tanah yang minta istirahat.

Energi sejati bukan pada minyak atau listrik, tapi pada niat baik yang menggerakkan peradaban. 

Inilah energi versi Mestakung, ketika teknologi, alam, dan manusia berjalan dalam satu frekuensi. 

 

Pertanian dan UMKM: Teknologi yang Berjiwa Manusia 

Bayangkan seorang petani di NTT. Ia memegang handphone sederhana. Di layar, AI berjiwa GASING berkata: 

“Tanam sedikit lebih dulu. Tanahmu sedang lelah. Besok baru kita tambah lagi, ya?” 

AI seperti ini bukan bos yang memerintah, tapi teman yang mengerti ritme alam dan hati petani. Ia membantu menghitung, memantau kelebaban tanah, membaca cuaca, 

tapi tidak pernah memaksa. 

Ia mengajak bertani dengan rasa, seperti orang tua mengajari anaknya menanam jagung. 

Untuk UMKM pun begitu. 

AI berjiwa GASING membantu pengrajin, pedagang kecil, nelayan, bukan dengan bahasa rumit, tapi dengan bahasa penuh empati: 

“Harga segini sudah adil.” 

“Kalau stok segini, kamu aman sampai minggu depan.”

Noken yang dibuat dengan cinta itu terbang jauh membawa cerita Papua.  

Di sinilah UMKM menjadi hidup: bukan sekadar jualan, melainkan resonansi budaya dan kasih yang tersebar lewat teknologi. 

  

Teknologi tidak sekadar mempermudah bisnis, tetapi menyuarakan budaya dan kasih yang yang ada di balik setiap produk.

Teknologi adalah cermin besar dari diri kita.

Bentuk karakter sebelum membentuk algoritma. 

Lindungi hati sebelum melindungi data.

sadar akan makna,

menambah kecerdasan kita,

Tuhan, Sang Kasih itu sendiri.

### 👤 User:
apakah and so that itu bisa diartikan agar ?

### 🤖 ChatGPT:
Ya, **“and so that” bisa diartikan “agar”**, tetapi tidak selalu merupakan padanan yang paling halus.

### **Penjelasan singkat:**

- **“so that”** = *agar / supaya*  
  (paling langsung, paling tepat untuk makna tujuan)

- **“and so that”** = *dan agar / dan supaya*  
  Digunakan ketika kalimat sebelumnya perlu disambungkan dulu.

Jadi, **secara makna** “and so that” memang bisa berarti **“dan agar”**, tetapi dalam konteks kalimat Anda, bentuk **“and so that”** bisa terdengar agak berat atau terlalu panjang.

Lebih natural jika ditulis:

### **“So that your love never runs dry…”**  
(*Agar kasihmu tidak habis…*)

Tetapi jika Anda memang ingin mempertahankan sambungan dari kalimat sebelumnya, maka:

### **“And so that your love never runs dry…”**  
(*Dan agar kasihmu tidak habis…*)

Tetap benar — hanya style-nya berbeda.

---

### ⭐ **Rekomendasi paling alami:**

**“So that your love never runs dry,  
you must return to its Source — the Creator.  
For God is Love.”**

Ini sederhana, kuat, dan sangat natural dalam bahasa Inggris.

### ⭐ **Pilihan 1 — Critical Point (paling pas dengan Mestakung)**  
In physics, every system approaches a *critical point*—a moment when the old structure can no longer survive, and a new form must emerge. At that threshold, the smallest disturbance can trigger a complete transformation. This is how nature evolves, not through force, but through readiness. Failure works the same way: it signals that we are reaching our own critical point of growth.

---

### ⭐ **Pilihan 2 — Self-Organizing Criticality**  
Modern physics calls this *self-organizing criticality*—the tendency of complex systems to reorganize themselves into a higher order after a period of chaos. Sand piles collapse into new stability. Forests regenerate after fire. Human life reorganizes after failure. Disorder is not destruction; it is reformation.

---

### ⭐ **Pilihan 3 — Entropy as Information (sangat elegan)**  
In contemporary physics, entropy is not just disorder—it is information. When things fall apart, new information enters the system, giving it the chance to rearrange itself more wisely. Failure, then, is simply new information arriving at the right time.

---

### ⭐ **Pilihan 4 — Resonansi dan alam semesta**  
Nature does not collapse randomly; it resonates toward balance. Every disruption is the universe nudging a system toward a more stable state. Failure is one such nudge—a resonance inviting us to find a better alignment.

---

terjemahkan: Bisnis yang Tumbuh Karena Jatuh 

Gramedia: Mestakung dalam Dunia Literasi 

Pada awal 1990-an, Gramedia berada di ambang krisis. Penjualan merosot, stok menumpuk di gudang, dan minat masyarakat bergeser ke hiburan elektronik. Secara logika bisnis, itu adalah jalan buntu. Tetapi justru di titik itulah hukum semesta bekerja. 

Krisis yang tampak seperti kegagalan besar ternyata menjadi sinyal pengaturan ulang, belokan tajam yang mengarahkan Gramedia menuju jalur baru. Mereka menyadari bahwa kelangsungan hidup tidak terletak pada menjual buku, tetapi pada merawat pengetahuan. 

Dari pemahaman itulah sinergi baru terbentuk. Gramedia mulai membangun ekosistem lengkap: penerbitan pendidikan, alat tulis, platform digital, pelatihan guru, komunitas literasi, hingga ruang belajar modern. Mereka tidak lagi berdiri sebagai toko buku, tetapi sebagai jembatan pengetahuan bangsa, menyentuh anak-anak SD hingga mahasiswa, guru hingga penulis. 

Pada titik tertentu, usaha keras itu mulai menarik dukungan dari segala arah, penulis bermunculan, komunitas tumbuh, sekolah-sekolah bekerja sama, dan masyarakat kembali menjadikan buku sebagai bagian dari hidup mereka. Inilah Mestakung bekerja di dunia nyata: ketika kegagalan finansial berubah menjadi fondasi bagi sinergi intelektual nasional. 

Krisis mendorong Gramedia menemukan bentuk terbaik dirinya. Dan ketika langkah-langkah kecil itu dijaga dengan tekun, semesta pun ikut membentangkan jalannya.

### **Businesses That Rise Because They Fall**

**Gramedia: Mestakung in the World of Literacy**

In the early 1990s, Gramedia stood on the edge of a crisis. Sales were dropping, warehouses were overloaded with unsold books, and public interest was shifting toward electronic entertainment. From a business perspective, it looked like a dead end.  
But it was precisely at that point that the laws of the universe began to work.

What seemed like a major failure turned out to be a signal for recalibration—a sharp turn redirecting Gramedia toward a new path.  
They realized that their survival did not lie in selling books, but in nurturing knowledge.

From that realization, a new synergy was born. Gramedia began building a complete ecosystem: educational publishing, stationery, digital platforms, teacher training, literacy communities, and modern learning spaces.  
They were no longer just a bookstore, but a bridge of knowledge for the nation—serving elementary children to university students, teachers to writers.

At a certain point, their hard work began to attract support from every direction.  
Writers appeared, communities grew, schools partnered with them, and society once again made books a part of daily life.  
This is Mestakung at work in the real world: when financial failure becomes the foundation for national intellectual synergy.

The crisis pushed Gramedia to discover its best form.  
And when those small steps were carried out with perseverance, the universe widened the road before them.

---

terjemahkan: IBM: Ketika Mestakung Bekerja dalam Skala Korporasi 

Pada awal 1990-an, IBM, raksasa komputer dunia, nyaris runtuh oleh kebesaran masa lalunya sendiri. Mereka masih bertumpu pada mainframe besar yang pernah menguasai dunia bisnis, sementara pasar global bergeser cepat ke PC dan jaringan terbuka. Biaya operasional membengkak, pangsa pasar jatuh, dan pada 1993 IBM mencatat kerugian terbesar dalam sejarah korporasi Amerika: US$8 miliar. Banyak analis menyebut IBM sebagai “dinosaurus teknologi” yang menunggu punah. 

Di tengah badai itu, datang Louis V. Gerstner Jr., seorang pemimpin yang bahkan bukan dari dunia teknologi. Ia tidak datang membawa visi muluk, hanya satu kalimat yang kini menjadi legendaris: “The last thing IBM needs right now is a vision. It needs to execute.” 

Gerstner memahami hukum momentum: IBM tidak kekurangan ide, mereka kekurangan langkah yang selaras. 

Langkah pertamanya bukan restrukturisasi radikal atau pergantian logo, tetapi mengubah cara berpikir puluhan ribu orang di dalamnya. Ketika banyak konsultan meminta IBM dipecah menjadi perusahaan-perusahaan kecil, Gerstner justru memilih jalan berlawanan. Ia menyatukan seluruh kekuatan IBM, memfokuskan energi mereka untuk menjadi penyedia solusi bisnis terpadu, bukan sekadar pembuat mesin. 

Ia menghidupkan kembali gemosi kolektif perusahaan, kepercayaan, kebanggaan, dan ritme kerja yang disiplin. Dalam bahasa Mestakung, Gerstner sedang menyetem kembali frekuensi IBM, agar resonansinya selaras dengan tuntutan zaman. 

Perubahan ini mengubah segalanya. 

Perlahan IBM meninggalkan identitas lamanya sebagai produsen perangkat keras dan menjelma menjadi mitra transformasi digital global. Mereka masuk ke layanan konsultasi, analitik, cloud computing, hingga kecerdasan buatan. IBM Watson menjadi simbol kebangkitan itu, IBM yang dulu kaku, kini menjadi pionir global AI enterprise. 

Transformasi ini bukan sekadar strategi bisnis; ini adalah contoh nyata Mestakung dalam skala korporasi. 

Ketika sebuah organisasi berani melepaskan ego masa lalu, menata langkah baru, menjaga gemosi kolektif, dan menyelaraskan diri dengan pola zaman, semesta pun membuka jalannya. Peluang, talenta, teknologi, dan dukungan global datang bergulir seperti energi yang menemukan jalur hambatan paling kecil. 

IBM tidak hanya selamat, IBM menemukan dirinya kembali. Dari mesin logam, mereka menemukan jiwa digital-nya. Inilah bentuk Mestakung dalam dunia bisnis: semesta mendukung mereka yang berani berubah dengan kesadaran. 

### 🤖 ChatGPT:
Berikut terjemahan yang **kuat, natural, dan tetap mempertahankan napas Mestakung**, tanpa mengurangi keakuratan sejarah:

---

### **IBM: When Mestakung Operates at a Corporate Scale**

In the early 1990s, IBM—the world’s computer giant—was nearly crushed by the weight of its own past success.  
They were still relying on massive mainframes that once dominated the business world, while the global market had rapidly shifted to PCs and open networks.  
Operational costs ballooned, market share collapsed, and in 1993 IBM recorded the largest loss in American corporate history: **US$8 billion**.  
Many analysts called IBM a “technological dinosaur” waiting to go extinct.

In the middle of this storm came Louis V. Gerstner Jr., a leader who wasn’t even from the tech world.  
He arrived not with a grand vision, but with one simple, now legendary sentence:

**“The last thing IBM needs right now is a vision. It needs to execute.”**

Gerstner understood the law of momentum: IBM did not lack ideas—what it lacked was aligned action.

His first step wasn’t a radical restructuring or a branding overhaul.  
It was changing the mindset of tens of thousands of people inside the company.  
While many consultants urged IBM to break itself into smaller companies, Gerstner chose the opposite path.  
He unified IBM’s strengths, focusing their energy on becoming an integrated provider of business solutions—not merely a machine builder.

He revived the company’s collective *gemosi*—its shared emotional rhythm—restoring trust, pride, and disciplined cadence.  
In the language of Mestakung, Gerstner was **retuning IBM’s frequency** so its resonance aligned with the demands of the era.

That shift changed everything.

Slowly, IBM left behind its old identity as a hardware manufacturer and transformed into a global partner for digital transformation.  
They expanded into consulting, analytics, cloud computing, and artificial intelligence.  
IBM Watson became the symbol of this rebirth—IBM, once rigid, emerging as a global pioneer in AI enterprise.

This transformation was not just business strategy;  
it was **a living example of Mestakung at the corporate scale**.

When an organization dares to release the ego of its past, set new steps, guard its collective *gemosi*, and align itself with the rhythm of the times, the universe opens the way.  
Opportunities, talent, technology, and global support begin to flow like energy finding the path of least resistance.

IBM did not merely survive—IBM rediscovered itself.  
From metal machines, it found its digital soul.

**This is Mestakung in the world of business:  
the universe supports those who dare to change with awareness.**

---

### **The Lesson from General Electric: Mestakung Through the Courage to Realign Energy**

There was a time when the name General Electric (GE) felt eternal.  
Founded by Thomas Edison in 1892, GE became an icon of American innovation: from electric lamps, jet engines, and household appliances to financial services.  
For decades, GE lived under an old belief: *the bigger the company, the stronger it becomes.*

But the universe has a gentle way of correcting such certainty.  
Entering the 2000s, GE began to lose its focus.  
It held hundreds of business lines that no longer supported one another.  
The company’s energy was spent not on innovation, but on sustaining a giant body that was becoming increasingly slow.

When the 2008 global financial crisis hit, the most important part of the conglomerate—GE Capital—was struck hard.  
Profits collapsed, debt piled up, and the stock price plummeted.  
GE did not fall because it was weak, but because it was **too big to move**.

This is where the Third Law of Mestakung, *Perseverance*, meets a new lesson:  
**the courage to realign energy.**

In 2021, under Larry Culp’s leadership, GE made a decision once thought unimaginable:  
to split itself into three independent companies—each more focused and agile.  
Its three core energies became:

- **GE Aerospace** (aviation)  
- **GE Vernova** (energy and power generation)  
- **GE HealthCare** (medical equipment)

This monumental restructuring was completed in 2024.  
The business world witnessed something remarkable:  
a giant on the verge of collapse transformed into three entities that were alive again—focused, agile, and breathing freely.

This transformation is Mestakung in another form.  
GE did not survive by becoming bigger, but by becoming aligned.  
Not by holding on to more, but by daring to let go.  
Not by moving faster, but by reorganizing its steps.

True synergy is not about putting everything into one container,  
but aligning what belongs together and releasing what does not.

In the language of Mestakung, this is the moment when the universe says:  
**“Realign your energy, and I will open a new path.”**

The lesson from GE applies to corporations, teams, and even personal life:

- Not everything that was once part of our journey must stay until the end.  
- Not every burden must be carried.  
- Not every old form deserves to be preserved.

What matters is not how much we hold onto,  
but how aligned our steps are with the direction of the universe.

GE grew again when it dared to stop being a “giant”  
and began becoming its new self.  
That is Mestakung: when the courage to realign, release, and simplify  
opens space for a far greater support from the universe.

terjemahkan: Kisah Perjalanan GASING 

Saya masih ingat masa-masa awal tahun 2000-an, ketika pertama kali mengembangkan metode GASING, Gampang, Asyik, Menyenangkan. Waktu itu saya melatih banyak anak Papua di Tangerang. Anak-anak itu polos, penuh semangat, dan punya rasa ingin tahu yang luar biasa. Saya melihat sesuatu di mata mereka, cahaya keyakinan bahwa mereka juga bisa. 

Hasilnya sungguh menggembirakan. Di ajang Olimpiade Sains Nasional tahun 2005, Provinsi Papua berhasil meraih dua medali emas, dan untuk pertama kalinya masuk peringkat delapan besar nasional. Saat itu hati saya bergetar. Saya tahu, jika anak-anak dari daerah yang jauh bisa berprestasi setinggi itu, berarti semua anak Indonesia pun bisa asalkan diberi cara belajar yang benar. Itulah momen di mana saya merasa, semesta sedang bekerja bersama saya. 

Pelatihan terus berlanjut hingga tahun 2011. Saya percaya, ketika kita menabur ilmu dengan cinta, hasilnya akan berlipat. Tapi seperti semua perjalanan besar, di balik sinar selalu ada bayangan. Tahun 2012, saya memutuskan untuk mendirikan Surya University, universitas yang saya impikan sebagai pusat pembaruan pendidikan Indonesia. Saya mengundang banyak diaspora hebat pulang ke tanah air, ingin membangun ekosistem pendidikan yang modern dan berjiwa Nusantara. 

Namun saya lupa satu hal penting yaitu mimpi besar tetap membutuhkan perhitungan yang matang. Semua mahasiswa saya beri beasiswa penuh, karena saya tidak tega melihat semangat mereka padam hanya karena biaya. Namun idealisme itu datang dengan harga mahal. Biaya operasional membengkak. Dalam dua sampai tiga tahun, semua tabungan habis. Saya mulai menjual aset satu per satu rumah, tanah, mobil, semua demi menutup hutang. Tapi jumlahnya terlalu besar. 

Akhirnya saya jatuh. Bukan hanya secara finansial, tapi juga secara batin. Saya merasa gagal. Pelatihan berhenti. Orang-orang yang dulu bersama saya perlahan menjauh. Saya seperti berdiri di tengah reruntuhan impian saya sendiri. Namun di saat saya nyaris menyerah, semesta kembali menunjukkan jalan. Tuhan menghadirkan seseorang yang menjadi titik balik, yaitu Bapak Luhut Binsar Pandjaitan. Dengan ketulusan yang luar biasa, beliau membantu saya keluar dari masa tersulit itu. Tahun 2021, beliau berkata, “Yohanes, lanjutkan GASING-mu. Fokus di pendidikan. Bawa ke Toba.” 

Kalimat itu sederhana, tapi mengguncang hati saya. Seolah saya diingatkan kembali pada panggilan sejati, bukan membangun gedung megah, melainkan membangun manusia. Sejak saat itu, saya memutuskan untuk kembali fokus pada pelatihan GASING. Sedikit demi sedikit, saya mulai mencicil hutang. Saya menyerahkan pengelolaan Surya University kepada seorang sahabat, mengalihkan STKIP Surya kepada Universitas Katolik Parahyangan (Unpar), dan keluar dari yayasan yang mengelola Sekolah GENIUS. Semua itu saya lakukan agar saya bisa memusatkan tenaga pada hal yang benar-benar membuat saya hidup kembali berada di ruang-ruang kelas, mendengar tawa para guru, dan melihat senyum para murid. 

Dan anehnya, ketika saya mulai fokus pada hal yang benar, beban itu terasa jauh lebih ringan. Seakan semesta berkata, “Akhirnya kamu kembali ke jalanmu.” Mestakung mulai bekerja lagi. Pemerintah daerah mulai kembali mengundang saya melatih guru-guru. Mereka melihat perubahan nyata yaitu murid yang dulu takut matematika kini senang dengan matematika dan belajar dengan percaya diri; guru yang dulu mengajar dengan rotan, kini mengajar dengan senyum dan kasih sayang. Kota Jayapura bahkan mengadakan pelatihan enam kali berturut-turut. Kini pelatihan GASING sudah menjangkau lebih dari 125 kabupaten/kota, bekerja sama dengan UPI, UNCEN, hingga TNI-AD. Bahkan, beberapa negara mulai tertarik belajar metode ini. 

Dari perjalanan panjang ini, saya belajar bahwa kegagalan tidak pernah datang untuk menghancurkan. Ia datang untuk mengembalikan kita ke pusat gravitasi kehidupan pada panggilan yang paling tulus di dalam diri. Ia adalah jembatan menuju versi terbaik dari diri dan karya kita. Saya jatuh bukan karena lemah, tapi karena semesta ingin saya kembali fokus pada hal yang membuat saya hidup yaitu membantu anak-anak Indonesia menemukan cahaya dalam belajar.  

Kini saya melihat bahwa perjalanan ini belum berakhir. Mestakung terus bekerja, dalam cara yang sering kali tak terduga. Setiap kali saya mengajar guru atau trainer, saya bisa merasakannya getaran lembut semesta yang berkata, “Lanjutkan. Kamu sudah di jalan yang benar.” 

 

Semua kisah yang kita lihat: IBM, Netflix, GE menunjukkan pola yang sama. Pola yang sederhana, tetapi sangat dalam: 

Kegagalan menyingkirkan kebisingan. 

Segala hal yang tidak penting akan rontok. Ego, asumsi lama, dan ambisi palsu terbakar habis. Yang tersisa hanyalah inti diri. 

Kegagalan memaksa kita kembali pada nilai sejati. 

Ketika semuanya jatuh, kita hanya punya dua pilihan: kembali ke apa yang benar-benar berarti, atau tenggelam dalam bayang-bayang. Kegagalan adalah alat semesta untuk mengingatkan kita pada pusat gravitasi hidup. 

Kegagalan membangunkan kreativitas yang tertidur. 

Justru ketika jalan lama tertutup, otak dipaksa membangun jalan baru. Di titik itulah inovasi muncul. Semua terobosan besar lahir dari situasi yang memaksa pikiran untuk meninggalkan zona aman. 

 

Dalam bahasa Mestakung, kegagalan adalah proses penyelarasan. Ia bukan hukuman, tetapi pemurnian sama seperti api yang mengubah besi mentah menjadi baja. 

Ketika sesuatu runtuh, bukan berarti semesta menjauh; justru semesta sedang membersihkan jalur agar energi kita lebih selaras dan lebih kuat. Ujian itu muncul bukan untuk menghentikan momentum, tetapi untuk mengukur ketekunan. Dan mereka yang tetap bertahan di tengah reruntuhan akan mendapati satu momen indah: Mestakung mulai bekerja lebih besar daripada sebelumnya. 

Karena setelah kebisingan hilang, nilai inti muncul, dan kreativitas bangkit, kita tidak lagi bergerak sendiri. Kita bergerak bersama semesta.

## **The Journey of GASING**

I still remember the early 2000s, when I first developed the GASING method—Gampang, Asyik, Menyenangkan (Simple, Fun, Enjoyable).  
At that time, I was training many Papuan children in Tangerang.  
They were innocent, eager, and incredibly curious.  
I saw something in their eyes—a spark of confidence that they *could* do it.

The results were astonishing.  
At the 2005 National Science Olympiad, Papua won two gold medals and, for the first time, ranked in the national top eight.  
My heart trembled.  
I knew then: if children from remote regions could achieve that level, then **all Indonesian children** could—if only we gave them the right way to learn.  
That was the moment I felt the universe working with me.

The training continued until 2011.  
I believed that when knowledge is sown with love, the results multiply.  
But like all great journeys, light always casts a shadow.

In 2012, I decided to establish Surya University, a university I dreamed would become a center for educational renewal in Indonesia.  
I invited many brilliant diaspora scholars to return home, hoping to build a modern education ecosystem rooted in the soul of the archipelago.

But I forgot one important truth: a big dream still requires careful calculation.  
I gave *every* student a full scholarship—I couldn’t bear to see their enthusiasm fade simply because of money.  
But that idealism came at a high cost.

Operational expenses soared.  
Within two to three years, all of my savings were gone.  
I began selling my assets one by one—houses, land, cars—everything, just to pay the debts.  
But the amount was overwhelming.

Eventually, I fell.  
Not only financially, but emotionally.  
I felt like a failure.  
The training stopped.  
People who once walked with me slowly drifted away.  
I stood among the ruins of my own dream.

And just when I was close to giving up, the universe showed the way again.  
God brought someone who became a turning point—**Mr. Luhut Binsar Pandjaitan**.  
With extraordinary sincerity, he helped me through my darkest period.  
In 2021, he said to me:  
**“Yohanes, continue your GASING. Focus on education. Bring it to Toba.”**

The sentence was simple, but it shook my heart.  
It reminded me of my true calling—not building grand buildings, but building human beings.

From that moment, I decided to refocus on GASING training.  
Little by little, I began paying the debts.  
I handed the management of Surya University to a close friend, transferred STKIP Surya to Parahyangan Catholic University (Unpar), and stepped away from the foundation that managed GENIUS School.  
I did all that so I could give my full strength to the one thing that brought me back to life:  
being in classrooms, hearing teachers laugh, and seeing students smile.

And strangely enough, when I returned to the right path, the burden became much lighter.  
It felt as if the universe whispered, *“At last, you are back on your true road.”*

Mestakung began to work again.  
Local governments started inviting me back to train teachers.  
They saw real change: students who once feared mathematics now enjoyed it and learned with confidence; teachers who once taught with a cane now taught with smiles and compassion.

Jayapura City even conducted six consecutive training cycles.  
GASING training has now reached over **125 districts/cities**, in collaboration with UPI, UNCEN, and even the Indonesian Army (TNI-AD).  
Several countries have also shown interest in learning the method.

From this long journey, I learned that failure never comes to destroy.  
It comes to bring us back to the gravitational center of life—to our truest calling.  
It is a bridge to the best version of ourselves and our work.

I did not fall because I was weak.  
I fell because the universe wanted me to return to the one thing that makes me come alive:  
**helping Indonesian children discover the light within learning.**

Today, I know the journey is far from over.  
Mestakung continues to work—in ways that often surprise me.  
Every time I train teachers or trainers, I can feel it:  
the gentle vibration of the universe saying,  
**“Continue. You are on the right path.”**

All the stories we have seen—IBM, Netflix, GE—show the same pattern.  
Simple, yet profoundly deep:

### **Failure removes the noise.**  
All things unnecessary fall away.  
Ego, old assumptions, and false ambitions burn to ashes.  
What remains is the core.

### **Failure pulls us back to our true values.**  
When everything collapses, we face two choices:  
return to what truly matters, or drown in the shadows.  
Failure is the universe’s tool to remind us of the gravitational center of life.

### **Failure awakens sleeping creativity.**  
Only when the old path closes does the mind build a new one.  
That is where innovation begins.  
Every great breakthrough is born from a moment that forces the mind out of its comfort zone.

In the language of Mestakung, **failure is alignment**.  
Not punishment, but purification—like fire turning raw iron into steel.

When something collapses, it does not mean the universe is leaving.  
It means the universe is clearing the path so our energy can move more clearly and more powerfully.

The test is not meant to stop our momentum,  
but to measure our perseverance.  
And those who stand firm among the ruins will eventually witness the most beautiful moment:

### **Mestakung begins to work bigger than before.**

Because once the noise fades,  
once the core values appear,  
once creativity awakens—

we no longer move alone.  
**We move with the universe.**

---
